\"\"\"\n    digits = _NON_DIGIT_RE.sub(\"\", phone or \"\").strip()\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:11]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 10:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:10]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 8:\n        return f\"{digits[0:4]}-{digits[4:8]}\"\n    return digits\n\n\n_PLACEHOLDER_RE =
